## What pass 2 found (the repair's inputs)

* codex: (A) latch suppression never revisited -- `|=`->`&=` predicted to keep every row green; (B) SPSR source
  labels: the restore rows ran from SVC only, while FIQ/IRQ/ABT/UND SPSRs also feed new_cpsr; probe overclaims
  (an `SP==0` claim with no readback; the cached-translation limit undisclosed).
* Opus 5.5 (MEASURED in its own clone): F1 deleting the `mode_warned |= ...` line at msr/pop/dpi/load each scored
  26/26; F2 `mask &= ~ARM_FLAG_MODE` -> `mask &= ~0x0f` scored 26/26 (every driven MSR value had bit 4 set);
  F3 = codex's (B).
* grok: the backstop comments' "only a debugger `cpsr=` reaches it" is false -- once a bad mode is planted, later
  GUEST instructions reach the backstops.
* records: the pass-2 brief pasted an ABORTED run's 25/26 score lines without marking them (kimi, deepseek).

## The repair

Product code: comments only changed over the pass-2 diff (the two backstop comments; the DPI comment).  The full
fix vs HEAD is inlined below (9 hunks).  The detector grew 26 -> 38 rows:
* `latch rpt` -- one process visits all five latch sites TWICE (msr, pop, dpi, then a debugger `cpsr=0xd4` plant
  and save/load), counts announces per site (exactly one each), reads CPU state back after the second visits;
* `src dpi|pop x fiq irq abt und svc` (10 rows) -- enter the mode by a modelled MSR, set that mode's SPSR to
  N|Z|0x14: restore 1 declined (whole CPSR word read back = `c00000XX`, mode kept); then SPSR_c := 0x1f, restore 2
  accepted (`c000001f`);
* `kill 03` -- `MSR CPSR_c,#0x03`, the only driven value with mode bit 4 clear;
* ` sp0` token requiring an actual zero SP readback on four rows; NOT CLAIMED list extended.

## The probe's contract (verbatim, regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py)

```
CONTRACT (floor clause (e)) -- the edits this detector is DESIGNED to catch:
  * exit(1) reinstated at Save's default: (dbg save, dbg both, latch rpt) or Load's (dbg both,
    latch rpt);
  * the helper answering 1 always (kill 14/15/1e), admitting 0x1c-0x1e (kill 1e), or dropping its
    `& ARM_FLAG_MODE' projection (src * rows: their SPSRs carry N and Z);
  * the guard dropped at msr_imm (kill rows), arm_pop (rst ldm [SVC], src pop x FIQ IRQ ABT UND
    SVC) or DPI (rst movs, zero spsr [SVC], src dpi x FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC), or restricted to ONE
    current-mode label (the src * rows: those five SPSR-bearing modes x both paths);
  * the msr_imm decline clearing fewer than all five mode bits, e.g. `& ~0x0f' (kill 03: the
    only driven mode value with bit 4 CLEAR);
  * a restore guard made unconditional, or unconditional for one label (acc movs, acc ldm, the
    valid-SPSR half of each src * row);
  * a WHOLE-OPERATION decline in msr_imm instead of field-only (the `if=' pin on kill rows);
  * the restore not taking N/Z/I/F from the SPSR (src * rows read the whole CPSR word);
  * any two of the five latch sites sharing a bit (latch rpt: all five in one process; also
    dbg both, latch ind);
  * a latch that never SUPPRESSES (`|=' -> `&=', or the `|=' line deleted), or a repair applied
    only while its latch is clear (latch rpt: every site visited TWICE, exactly ONE announce each,
    repair still applied).
NOT CLAIMED, disclosed: unmodelled values other than 0x00, 0x14, 0x15, 0x1e (a helper wrong only
at 0x16, 0x18-0x1a, 0x1c-0x1d or 0x01-0x0f escapes); the msr_imm comment's "only the LOCAL mask
changes: ic->arg[] is a cached translation" -- no row re-executes ONE translated MSR, so a guard
that wrote ic->arg[] would go unseen; per-CPU latch isolation (one CPU only); T-bit /
execution-state coherence on restore; the USR/SYS `LDM {pc}^' and `MSR SPSR' paths that stop the
CPU (filed separately).

NAMED-RED tally: HEAD = 16/38, RED exactly kill 14, kill 15, save 2s, kill 1e, kill 03, rst movs,
rst ldm,
zero spsr, dbg save, dbg both, latch ind, latch rpt, and the ten src * rows, CONTROL=OK.  `save
2s' on HEAD is the SAME Load death as `kill 14' (EOF on the first MSR) -- it is not an
independent HEAD reproduction of Save; nor is `dbg both' a HEAD reproduction of Load (HEAD dies
in Save first).  38/38 on HEAD
is a broken vehicle, not success.

```

   Five latch sites, each now visited TWICE in one process (latch rpt), count-checked (==1), with a
   readback after the second visit.  Two restore paths x five SPSR-bearing labels, each with a
   declined and an accepted cell in one session (src dpi/pop x fiq irq abt und svc).  Remaining
   asymmetry: the SPSR source label is crossed with the reserved value 0x14 only, the zero-SPSR
   cell stays SVC/DPI only (disclosed in the header as values-other-than / one CPU).

   cpu_arm.h "each announce once per CPU, independently": latch rpt (five sites, x2, count==1).
              "per CPU": NOT rowed (testarm has one CPU) -- disclosed.
   Save default (reworded) "reached only once an unmodelled mode is ALREADY in CPSR -- then any
              later bank switch (guest MSR, S-to-PC, exception entry)": dbg save (guest MSR),
              dbg both + latch rpt (guest MOVS).  Exception entry: NOT rowed (single-step
              suppresses IRQ dispatch; no SWI/UND row) -- disclosed by omission, noted here.
   Load default (reworded): dbg both, latch rpt.

              readback c0000011.. / c000001f).  "T forced for an odd destination PC": NOT rowed.
   arm_pop "every other bit comes from the SPSR": src pop * (whole word).
   msr_imm "only the local mask changes: ic->arg[] is a cached translation": NOT rowed -- now in the
              header's NOT CLAIMED list.
   probe acc usr/acc movs/acc ldm/dbg save "SP is 0": ` sp0' requires dumped == 0.

3. GUARD FIELD vs BODY FIELD
   Restore guards read new_cpsr (selected by the CURRENT mode label: spsr_fiq/irq/svc/abt/und);
   the body writes cpsr from new_cpsr and decides the bank switch on it.  The selector (current
   mode) flows into new_cpsr -> per the selector-data-flow rule each label is an axis: rowed x
   {declined, accepted} x {DPI, POP}.  Helper reads `mode & ARM_FLAG_MODE'; the src SPSRs carry
   N|Z in bits 31:30 so the projection's operand has a nonzero high part.
   Latch field: the five `|=' writers; latch rpt reads every bit's suppression and independence.

4. PER MUTANT: predicted killing row and the differing observable
   N_latch_and_{msr,pop,dpi,save,load}   latch rpt: msr2 / pop2 / dpi2 / save2 / load2
   N_cond_msr     latch rpt: 2nd MSR (#0x15) applied -> s_14 in mode 0x15 -> msr_imm_spsr default
                  (running=0) -> a=nofire (or dead)
   N_cond_pop     latch rpt: 2nd LDM^ applies 0x14 -> Load backstop -> a=14/.. + load text
   N_cond_dpi     latch rpt: 2nd MOVS applies 0x14 -> a=14/.., load text
   N_cond_save    latch rpt: 2nd Save visit -> exit(1) -> dead
   N_cond_load    latch rpt: 2nd Load visit -> exit(1) -> dead
   N_dpi_svc_only src dpi {fiq,irq,abt,und}: c1 = c0000014 (applied), Load backstop
   N_pop_svc_only src pop {fiq,irq,abt,und}: same
   N_dpi_keep_irq src dpi irq: c2 = c0000012 not c000001f
   N_pop_keep_abt src pop abt: c2 = c0000017 not c000001f
   N_helper_nomask  every src * row: c2 = c00000XX (valid SPSR declined because N|Z is set);
                  also ` once' count may change
   N_alias_save_msr latch rpt: save0 (Save's text suppressed by the msr decline's bit)
   M_* (table-1 set, re-run on the 37-row probe as regression): same killing rows as table 1,
   plus latch rpt where the edit touches a latch site or a backstop.
   CHANNEL check: every listed observable is a readback word, an exact announce COUNT or alive/

 /*
+ *  arm_mode_is_modelled():
+ *
+ *  #562: 1 if M[4:0] of `mode' is one of the seven modes this model banks
+ *  (10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f), else 0.  Its case list MUST stay equal to the
+ *  case lists of arm_save_register_bank() and arm_load_register_bank()
+ *  below: the three switches together define "modelled", and those two
+ *  default: arms are reached only for a mode this returns 0 for.  Extern,
+ *  not static: the DPI template is compiled as its own object.
+ */
+int arm_mode_is_modelled(uint32_t mode)
+{
+	switch (mode & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
+	case ARM_MODE_USR32:
+	case ARM_MODE_SYS32:
+	case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
+	case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
+	case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
+	case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
+	case ARM_MODE_UND32:
+		return 1;
+	default:return 0;
+	}
+}

## Questions -- answer each `<n> | <answer>`

1. FLOOR, asked of the SHIPPED COMPOSITE (not only the repair hunks): is there a single smallest edit to the fix
   that keeps all 38 rows green?  Enumerate the cells you swept (the denominator) -- "zero found" must say what
   was looked for.  Consider: the guard dominating predicates in msr_imm / arm_pop / DPI, the five latch sites,
   the helper, the `spsr_copied` gate, and each restore path's label axis.
2. The 2026-09-09 label clause: the DPI/pop restore selects new_cpsr by the CURRENT mode label.  Is the rowed
   product {fiq irq abt und svc} x {declined, accepted} x {dpi, pop} the right closure, or is a cell missing
   (e.g. the zero-SPSR cell rowed for SVC/DPI only; latch state x label)?
3. Records: is any comment, contract line or tally above false?  In particular the reworded backstop comments,
   the DPI comment's "T is forced after Load for an odd destination PC", and the NOT CLAIMED list.
4. Is the NOT CLAIMED list honest -- does any disclosed gap hide something a guest reaches on testarm that the
   round claims to have fixed?
**If, while verifying, you found something ELSE -- say so.**


## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""armmsrmodekill -- RUNG-3 WITNESS.  One privileged MSR ends the PROCESS on `-E testarm'.

BUILT FROM regress/artifacts/armmsrmodekill/WITNESSSPEC_REWRITE_grok_20260917.txt, which is a
REWRITE and not the original 16-row spec plus eight sticky notes.  That spec's amendments file
carries a SUPERSEDED banner; do not build from it.

REPAIRED after regress/artifacts/armmsrmodekill/PROBEATTACK_grok_20260917.txt (the attack on the
never-run first draft) -- see the `REPAIR' notes inline.  Summary of the repairs:
  R1  dump parsing: aligned base + little-endian assembly (read_mem32() in arm_bdt_probe.py,
      sw() in arm_flags_probe.py).  The first draft already carried both; kept.
  R2  EARLY/LATE SPLIT.  Every MSR session dumps the 0x9000 line TWICE (the arming dump of DEST
      before the stimulus, the result dump after it).  A first-match parse returns the EARLY
      line, where every post-MSR slot is still POISON -- so a correct fix would have read
      `nofire' on every kill row.  An `@MARK' pseudo-command becomes `print 0x5eed5eed' and
      the transcript is split at that token; arming is parsed only from before it, results
      only from after it.
  R3  `save 2s' plant and classifier agree, on the spec's own slots: return CPSR at DEST+8,
      SP at DEST+12, both inside the one dumped line.  The first draft planted SP at DEST+16
      and never dumped 0x9010, so ` sp' could never appear -- the row was ALWAYS red.  `rt'
      requires mode == 0x13 at the return slot, not mere existence.
  R4  the plant-dump of the MSR slot is SCORED: every MSR session dumps its whole planted
      stream before the stimulus and checks each word against WORDS, plus DEST == 0xd3 (the
      spec's "every later session repeats DEST==0xd3 + plant-dump").  A failure renders the
      row `unarmed', never `dead'.  This is what separates "died at the MSR" from "the MSR was
      never the instruction that ran".
  R5  CONTROL=DEAD stops the probe: `enc ok' red stops before any guest session; `live' or
      `pc adv' red stops before `armed'; `armed' red stops before any kill row.
  R6  `enc ok' note quoted the removed (w & 0x0FB0F000) mask; it now states the field-nibble
      test the code performs.  f_14 additionally checks rot == 0.
  R7  liveness: EOF on the pty is recorded; on EOF the child is reaped with a long deadline,
      without EOF with a short one (a live emulator never exits, and every live session used to
      burn the full 12 s).  The SIGKILL tail now READS THE STATUS: if waitpid shows the child
      exited on its own (not by our SIGKILL), alive is False.
  R8  canary accept rows also check arrival SP == 0 (spec: "arrive m=XX SP=0"), and `acc usr'
      now passes sp_at so a Load-skip (SP still BOOT_SP in USR32) reds it.

THE DEFECT, verified by reading cpu_arm.c at HEAD ef577140 -- PRE-#562; #562 replaced both exits
with latched backstops, so these lines describe the unfixed tree only:

    cpu_arm.c:606-608   arm_load_register_bank: default: fatal("...unimplemented mode %i"); exit(1);
    cpu_arm.c:559-561   arm_save_register_bank: default: same

`0xE321F014' is `MSR CPSR_c, #0x14' -- mode 0x14 is a RESERVED ARM mode, so the Load arm's
`default:' is reached and the process dies.  `%i' prints DECIMAL: `unimplemented mode 20'.

*** POLARITY: WANT IS POST-FIX CORRECT.  A PRE FAIL ON A DEFECT ROW *IS* THE REPRODUCTION. ***
*** ORACLE: EOF ALONE => DEAD.  THE TEXT IS DIAGNOSIS, NEVER HALF THE TRIGGER. ***

#562 THE FIX AND WHAT THIS PROBE NOW DETECTS.  Three guest writers can put an unmodelled mode
(anything but 10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f) into CPSR, and each now DECLINES THE MODE FIELD ONLY, with a
`(once)' announce naming its site: msr_imm (`MSR CPSR_c'), arm_pop's `LDM {..pc}^' SPSR restore,
and the DPI `MOVS pc,..' SPSR restore.  The restores keep the CURRENT mode in M[4:0] and assign
every other bit from the SPSR (DPI later forces T for an odd destination PC; not driven here).
The Save/Load `default:' arms are BACKSTOPS (latched announce, return, no exit(1)), reached only
once an unmodelled mode is ALREADY in CPSR -- then by any later bank switch, including a guest
`MSR CPSR_c' to a modelled mode (dbg save) or `MOVS pc,lr' (dbg both); no guest instruction can
now install such a mode, only a debugger `cpsr=' assignment.  POST-FIX the guest is in SVC32 with
the I/F bits the write set -- `#0x14' clears I and F -- NOT in a reserved mode.  Rows added by
#562: kill 1e, kill 03, rst movs, rst ldm, zero spsr, acc movs, acc ldm, dbg save, dbg both, latch ind,
latch rpt, src dpi/src pop x {fiq irq abt und svc}, the `if=' (I/F) pin on kill 14 / kill 15,
and ` sp0' (an actual zero SP readback) on the rows whose SP is a never-loaded bank.

MODES 0x00-0x0F ARE NOW CLAIMED.  This header used to BAN `MSR CPSR_c, #0' as "the 26-bit hole,
a different defect".  The helper declines 0x00-0x0F like any other unmodelled value, and
`zero spsr' drives exactly mode 0x00 (an SPSR never written is zero: cpu_new zero-fills).

CONTRACT (floor clause (e)) -- the edits this detector is DESIGNED to catch:
  * exit(1) reinstated at Save's default: (dbg save, dbg both, latch rpt) or Load's (dbg both,
    latch rpt);
  * the helper answering 1 always (kill 14/15/1e), admitting 0x1c-0x1e (kill 1e), or dropping its
    `& ARM_FLAG_MODE' projection (src * rows: their SPSRs carry N and Z);
  * the guard dropped at msr_imm (kill rows), arm_pop (rst ldm [SVC], src pop x FIQ IRQ ABT UND
    SVC) or DPI (rst movs, zero spsr [SVC], src dpi x FIQ IRQ ABT UND SVC), or restricted to ONE
    current-mode label (the src * rows: those five SPSR-bearing modes x both paths);
  * the msr_imm decline clearing fewer than all five mode bits, e.g. `& ~0x0f' (kill 03: the
    only driven mode value with bit 4 CLEAR);
  * a restore guard made unconditional, or unconditional for one label (acc movs, acc ldm, the
    valid-SPSR half of each src * row);
  * a WHOLE-OPERATION decline in msr_imm instead of field-only (the `if=' pin on kill rows);
  * the restore not taking N/Z/I/F from the SPSR (src * rows read the whole CPSR word);
  * any two of the five latch sites sharing a bit (latch rpt: all five in one process; also
    dbg both, latch ind);
  * a latch that never SUPPRESSES (`|=' -> `&=', or the `|=' line deleted), or a repair applied
    only while its latch is clear (latch rpt: every site visited TWICE, exactly ONE announce each,
    repair still applied).
NOT CLAIMED, disclosed: unmodelled values other than 0x00, 0x14, 0x15, 0x1e (a helper wrong only
at 0x16, 0x18-0x1a, 0x1c-0x1d or 0x01-0x0f escapes); the msr_imm comment's "only the LOCAL mask
changes: ic->arg[] is a cached translation" -- no row re-executes ONE translated MSR, so a guard
that wrote ic->arg[] would go unseen; per-CPU latch isolation (one CPU only); T-bit /
execution-state coherence on restore; the USR/SYS `LDM {pc}^' and `MSR SPSR' paths that stop the
CPU (filed separately).

NAMED-RED tally: HEAD = 16/38, RED exactly kill 14, kill 15, save 2s, kill 1e, kill 03, rst movs,
rst ldm,
zero spsr, dbg save, dbg both, latch ind, latch rpt, and the ten src * rows, CONTROL=OK.  `save
2s' on HEAD is the SAME Load death as `kill 14' (EOF on the first MSR) -- it is not an
independent HEAD reproduction of Save; nor is `dbg both' a HEAD reproduction of Load (HEAD dies
in Save first).  38/38 on HEAD
is a broken vehicle, not success.

Stay on `step', never `continue' (IRQ dispatch is inside `if (!single_step)').
"""
import os
import pty
import re
import select
import signal
import sys
import time

BIN = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "../build/gxemul"
LABEL = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "?"

STUB = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "arm_bfx_stub.bin")
if not os.path.exists(STUB):
    with open(STUB, "wb") as f:
        f.write(b"\0" * 16)

CODE = 0x8000
DEST = 0x9000
BOOT_SP = 0x03FFF000
BOOT_CPSR = 0xD3
POISON = 0xDEADBEEF
MARK = "@MARK"

#  *** ONE WORDS DICT.  Every `put w' is drawn from here and every MSR session dumps its whole
#  planted stream against these entries before the stimulus (R4). ***
WORDS = {
    "mrs_r3":   0xE10F3000,              # mrs  r3, cpsr
    "str_r3":   0xE5843000,              # str  r3, [r4]
    "str_r3_4": 0xE5843004,              # str  r3, [r4,#4]
    "str_r3_8": 0xE5843008,              # str  r3, [r4,#8]
    "str_r3_c": 0xE584300C,              # str  r3, [r4,#12]
    "str_sp_8": 0xE584D008,              # str  r13,[r4,#8]
    "str_sp_c": 0xE584D00C,              # str  r13,[r4,#12]
    "str_sp_10": 0xE584D010,             # str  r13,[r4,#16]
    "mov_r0_2a": 0xE3A0002A,             # mov  r0, #0x2a
    "str_r0":   0xE5840000,              # str  r0, [r4]
    "b_self":   0xEAFFFFFE,              # b    .
}
for m in (0x03, 0x10, 0x11, 0x12, 0x13, 0x14, 0x15, 0x17, 0x1B, 0x1E, 0x1F):
    WORDS["c_%02x" % m] = 0xE321F000 | m     # msr cpsr_c, #m
WORDS["f_14"] = 0xE328F014                   # msr cpsr_f, #0x14
#  #562: SPSR poisoning and the two restore paths.
for m in (0x14, 0x1F):
    WORDS["s_%02x" % m] = 0xE361F000 | m     # msr spsr_c, #m
WORDS["movs_pc_lr"] = 0xE1B0F00E             # movs pc, lr       (DPI S-to-PC restore)
WORDS["ldm_r5_pc_s"] = 0xE8D58000            # ldmia r5, {pc}^   (arm_pop restore)

TGT = 0x8100                                 # restore target: lr, and the word at LDMW
LDMW = 0x9100                                # where `ldm_r5_pc_s' loads pc from
TGT_OPS = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"]
TGT2 = 0x8140                                # `latch ind': readback after the chained MOVS
#  #562 pass 2: words for `latch rpt' and the `src *' rows.
WORDS["ldm_r6_pc_s"] = 0xE8D68000            # ldmia r6, {pc}^
WORDS["mov_lr_8100"] = 0xE3A0EC81            # mov  lr, #0x8100  (0x81 ror 24)
WORDS["add_lr_40"] = 0xE28EE040              # add  lr, lr, #0x40
WORDS["sf_c0"] = 0xE368F103                  # msr  spsr_f, #0xc0000000  (N and Z; 0x03 ror 2)
NZ = 0xC0000000

rows = []
diagnosis = []


def row(name, got, want, note=""):
    rows.append((name, str(got), str(want), note))


def session(cmds, settle=1.6, spin=1.6):
    """ONE FRESH PROCESS per scored row that executes a guest instruction.  Returns (t, alive);
    _session_split() turns the `@MARK' pseudo-command into the early/late split (R2)."""
    pid, fd = pty.fork()
    if pid == 0:
        os.execv(BIN, [BIN, "-V", "-A", "-E", "testarm", "-M", "64",
                       "0x%x:%s" % (CODE, STUB)])
        os._exit(127)
    buf = ""
    eof = False

    def rd(t):
        nonlocal buf, eof
        end = time.time() + t
        while time.time() < end:
            try:
                r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
            except OSError:
                eof = True
                return
            if not r:
                continue
            try:
                d = os.read(fd, 65536)
            except OSError:
                eof = True
                return
            if not d:
                eof = True
                return
            buf += d.decode("utf-8", "replace")

    rd(settle)
    for c in cmds:
        if eof:
            break
        try:
            os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode())
        except OSError:
            eof = True
            break
        rd(0.3)
    rd(spin)

    #  *** POLL, NEVER SAMPLE ONCE. ***  rd() returns immediately on EOF, so a dying child must
    #  be given time to die.  On EOF reap with a long deadline; without EOF a short one (R7).
    alive = True
    deadline = time.time() + (12.0 if eof else 3.0)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        try:
            wpid, _st = os.waitpid(pid, os.WNOHANG)
        except ChildProcessError:
            alive = False
            break
        if wpid == pid:
            alive = False
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    if alive:
        try:
            os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            _w, st = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
            #  R7: if the child exited on its own between the last poll and our kill, the
            #  status says so -- do not leave alive=True for a process that died by itself.
            if os.WIFEXITED(st) or (os.WIFSIGNALED(st) and os.WTERMSIG(st) != signal.SIGKILL):
                alive = False
        except ChildProcessError:
            pass
    try:
        os.close(fd)
    except OSError:
        pass
    return buf.replace("\r", ""), alive


def _session_split(cmds):
    #  Insert a unique, harmless marker command at MARK so the split survives \r stripping:
    #  `print 0x5eed5eed' is answered by the debugger (sh_intprio_probe.py measured `print
    #  0x1234'), and even if it were not, its echo carries the token.
    real = []
    for c in cmds:
        real.append("print 0x5eed5eed" if c == MARK else c)
    t, alive = session(real)
    i = t.find("5eed5eed")
    if i < 0:
        return t, "", alive, False
    #  late = everything after the marker's answer line (skip echo + answer)
    j = t.rfind("5eed5eed")
    nl = t.find("\n", j)
    late = t[nl + 1:] if nl >= 0 else ""
    return t[:i], late, alive, True


def printed(t, reg):
    #  COPIED VERBATIM from regress/lca_isalane_probe.py.  `print <reg>' emits a BARE `0x...'.
    m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
    return None if not m else int(m.group(1), 16)


def dumpcmd(addr, end=None):
    """*** ALWAYS DUMP FROM THE 16-BYTE-ALIGNED BASE. ***  debugger_cmd_dump does
    `addr = addr_start & ~0xf' and blank-pads groups before addr_start (arm_bdt_probe.py
    read_mem32(), measured 2026-09-11)."""
    base = addr & ~0xF
    top = ((end if end is not None else addr) + 16) & ~0xF
    if top <= base:
        top = base + 16
    return "dump 0x%x 0x%x" % (base, top)


def dumped(t, addr):
    """The word stored at `addr', from the LAST aligned dump line in `t' (R2: callers pass the
    early or the late half, never the whole transcript).  Memory-order bytes, little-endian
    guest: assemble like sw() in arm_flags_probe.py."""
    label = "0x%08x" % (addr & ~0xF)
    idx = (addr & 0xF) // 4
    val = None
    for line in t.splitlines():
        if line.startswith(label):
            parts = line[12:48].split()
            if len(parts) == 4 and len(parts[idx]) == 8:
                val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(parts[idx]), "little")
    return val


def plant(ops, at=CODE):
    out = []
    for i, k in enumerate(ops):
        out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, WORDS[k]))
    out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), WORDS["b_self"]))
    return out


def prologue():
    """POISON DEST..DEST+16 (the post-MSR stores must overwrite them) and point r4 at DEST."""
    return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (DEST + o, POISON) for o in (0, 4, 8, 12, 16)] \
        + ["r4=0x%x" % DEST]


PREFIX = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3"]


def msr_cmds(ops, steps_after, late_end=DEST + 12, restore=False, regs=(), poke=(),
             chain=False):
    """Standard MSR session: prefix MRS/STR to DEST, arming dumps (DEST and the whole planted
    stream), MARK, the stimulus steps, then the result dump.
    #562: restore=True also plants TGT_OPS at TGT, sets lr=TGT and the LDMW word to TGT, and
    arms all three (a fall-through past the restore lands on b_self and reads `nofire').
    `regs' are debugger assignments made BEFORE the prefix; `poke' ones AFTER the arming dumps
    (a `cpsr=' poke must follow the prefix, whose MRS arms DEST == 0xd3).
    chain=True (with restore): TGT holds `movs pc,lr' and the readback TGT_OPS move to TGT2,
    where lr points -- so one session can run an LDM^ restore and then a MOVS restore."""
    full = PREFIX + ops
    cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
    extra = {}
    rb = TGT2 if chain else TGT
    if restore:
        cmds += plant(TGT_OPS, at=rb) + ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (LDMW, TGT),
                                         "lr=0x%x" % rb, "r5=0x%x" % LDMW]
        for i, k in enumerate(TGT_OPS + ["b_self"]):
            extra[rb + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
        extra[LDMW] = TGT
        if chain:
            cmds += plant(["movs_pc_lr"], at=TGT)
            extra[TGT] = WORDS["movs_pc_lr"]
            extra[TGT + 4] = WORDS["b_self"]
    cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
                          dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
    if restore:
        cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT, TGT + 4 * len(TGT_OPS)), dumpcmd(LDMW)]
        if chain:
            cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT2, TGT2 + 4 * len(TGT_OPS))]
    cmds += [MARK] + list(poke) + ["step %d" % s for s in steps_after] \
        + [dumpcmd(DEST, late_end)]
    return cmds, (full, extra)


def arming(early, full):
    """R4: DEST == 0xd3 after the prefix, and every planted word reads back as its WORDS entry.
    #562: `full' may be (ops, {addr: word}) -- the extra plants (restore target, LDM word)."""
    extra = {}
    if isinstance(full, tuple):
        full, extra = full
    if dumped(early, DEST) != BOOT_CPSR:
        return False
    for i, k in enumerate(full):
        if dumped(early, CODE + 4 * i) != WORDS[k]:
            return False
    for a, w in extra.items():
        if dumped(early, a) != w:
            return False
    return True


def prog_session(code_ops, blocks=(), data=None, regs=(), segments=((), ()), late_end=DEST + 12):
    """#562 pass 2: a multi-block program.  code_ops at CODE (after PREFIX), each (addr, ops) block
    at addr, each {addr: word} data word planted; every block and data word is ARMED (dumped and
    checked before MARK).  `segments' = ((pokes, steps), ...) after MARK: debugger assignments,
    then `step N'."""
    data = data or {}
    full = PREFIX + list(code_ops)
    cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
    extra = {}
    for a, ops in blocks:
        cmds += plant(ops, at=a)
        for i, k in enumerate(list(ops) + ["b_self"]):
            extra[a + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
    for a, w in data.items():
        cmds.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (a, w))
        extra[a] = w
    cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
                          dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
    for a, ops in blocks:
        cmds.append(dumpcmd(a, a + 4 * len(ops)))
    for a in data:
        cmds.append(dumpcmd(a))
    cmds.append(MARK)
    for pokes, steps in segments:
        cmds += list(pokes) + (["step %d" % steps] if steps else [])
    cmds.append(dumpcmd(DEST, late_end))
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    return early, late, alive, marked, (full, extra)


def announces(t, key):
    """#562 pass 2: how MANY `(once)' lines the named site printed -- a latch that never
    suppresses prints twice when its site is visited twice; any() could not see that."""
    return sum(1 for ln in t.splitlines() if key in ln and "(once)" in ln)


def diag_unimpl(nm, t):
    """#562: which register-bank `unimplemented mode' text a session printed (HEAD: the
    function that exit(1)ed -- the reason under test; fixed: the backstop that announced)."""
    hits = sorted(set(re.findall(r"arm_(save|load)_register_bank: unimplemented mode (\d+)", t)))
    diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, ", ".join("%s mode %s" % h for h in hits) if hits
                                 else "no bank text"))


def announced(t, key):
    """#562: a `(once)' announce from the NAMED site -- the five sites share the token, so a
    bare `(once)' cannot tell a decline from a backstop, or Save's backstop from Load's."""
    return any(key in ln and "(once)" in ln for ln in t.splitlines())


def classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, cpsr_at=DEST + 4, sp_at=None, rt_at=None,
             once_key=None, pin_if=False, backstops=False, declines=False):
    """The got string.  EOF ALONE => dead.  Arming is checked first, from the EARLY half.
    #562: once_key names the announcing site; pin_if adds the CPSR I/F pair (field-only vs
    whole-operation decline); backstops adds ` save' / ` load' per Save/Load announce."""
    if not marked or not arming(early, full):
        return "unarmed"
    if not alive:
        return "dead"
    cpsr = dumped(late, cpsr_at)
    if cpsr is None or cpsr == POISON:
        return "nofire"
    got = "m=%02x" % (cpsr & 0x1F)
    if pin_if:
        got += " if=%d" % ((cpsr >> 6) & 3)
    if sp_at is not None:
        v = dumped(late, sp_at)
        #  #562 pass 2: ` sp0' is an ACTUAL zero readback (a never-loaded bank), not merely
        #  "not BOOT_SP" -- a missing or POISON slot now renders neither token.
        if v == BOOT_SP:
            got += " sp"
        elif v == 0:
            got += " sp0"
    t = early + late
    if (announced(t, once_key) if once_key else "(once)" in t):
        got += " once"
    if declines:
        for key, tag in (("msr_imm", "msr"), ("arm_pop", "pop"), ("dpi S-to-pc", "dpi")):
            if announced(t, key):
                got += " " + tag
    if backstops:
        if announced(t, "arm_save_register_bank"):
            got += " save"
        if announced(t, "arm_load_register_bank"):
            got += " load"
    if rt_at is not None:
        v = dumped(late, rt_at)
        if v not in (None, POISON) and (v & 0x1F) == 0x13:
            got += " rt"
    return got


def report_dead(why):
    print("arm_msrmode_probe -- binary=%s label=%s machine=testarm" % (BIN, LABEL))
    print("ARMMSRMODE_CONTROL=DEAD")
    print("ARMMSRMODE_RESULT=0/0")
    print("  *** SETUP FAILED (%s) -- the vehicle is not the vehicle.  NOT 'no defect'. ***" % why)
    for nm, got, want, note in rows:
        print("  %-11s got=%-16s want=%-16s %s" % (nm, got, want, note[:56]))
    print("ARMMSRMODE_DONE")
    raise SystemExit(0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- enc ok  (no guest)
#  MSR immediate:  cond 00110 R 10 field(19:16) 1111 rot(11:8) imm8.  The field nibble is
#  bits 19:16 and a mask cannot substitute for it (0x0FB0F000 kills bit 0x08 and cannot tell
#  the c-form from the f-form).
bad = []
for k, w in WORDS.items():
    if k.startswith("c_"):
        if ((w >> 16) & 0xF) != 0b0001 or (w & 0xFF) != int(k[2:], 16) \
                or ((w >> 8) & 0xF) != 0 or (w & 0x00400000) or (w & 0x0FB0F000) != 0x0320F000:
            bad.append(k)
    if k == "f_14":
        if ((w >> 16) & 0xF) != 0b1000 or (w & 0xFF) != 0x14 or ((w >> 8) & 0xF) != 0 \
                or (w & 0x00400000) or (w & 0x0FB0F000) != 0x0320F000:
            bad.append(k)
    if k.startswith("s_"):
        #  #562: the SPSR form -- same frame, field nibble 0001, bit 22 (R) SET.
        if ((w >> 16) & 0xF) != 0b0001 or (w & 0xFF) != int(k[2:], 16) \
                or ((w >> 8) & 0xF) != 0 or not (w & 0x00400000) \
                or (w & 0x0FB0F000) != 0x0320F000:
            bad.append(k)
    if k == "movs_pc_lr":
        #  data-processing MOV (opcode 1101), S set, Rd = 15, Rm = 14, no shift
        if (w >> 21) & 0xF != 0xD or not (w >> 20) & 1 or (w >> 12) & 0xF != 15 \
                or w & 0xFFF != 14 or (w >> 25) & 7 != 0:
            bad.append(k)
    if k in ("ldm_r6_pc_s", "mov_lr_8100", "add_lr_40", "sf_c0"):
        #  #562 pass 2: whole-word field decode against the intended operation.
        ok = {"ldm_r6_pc_s": (w >> 25) & 7 == 0b100 and (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 22) & 1
              and not (w >> 21) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 6 and w & 0xFFFF == 0x8000,
              "mov_lr_8100": (w >> 25) & 7 == 1 and (w >> 21) & 0xF == 0xD
              and not (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 14
              and (((w & 0xFF) >> (2 * ((w >> 8) & 0xF))) | ((w & 0xFF)
                   << (32 - 2 * ((w >> 8) & 0xF)))) & 0xFFFFFFFF == TGT,
              "add_lr_40": (w >> 25) & 7 == 1 and (w >> 21) & 0xF == 0x4
              and not (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 14 and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 14
              and w & 0xFFF == 0x40,
              "sf_c0": (w & 0x0FB0F000) == 0x0320F000 and (w & 0x00400000)
              and ((w >> 16) & 0xF) == 0b1000 and ((w >> 8) & 0xF) == 1 and (w & 0xFF) == 3,
              }[k]
        if not ok:
            bad.append(k)
    if k == "ldm_r5_pc_s":
        #  LDM: bits 27:25 = 100, L set, S (bit 22) set, W clear, Rn = 5, list = {pc} only
        if (w >> 25) & 7 != 0b100 or not (w >> 20) & 1 or not (w >> 22) & 1 \
                or (w >> 21) & 1 or (w >> 16) & 0xF != 5 or w & 0xFFFF != 0x8000:
            bad.append(k)
row("enc ok", "clean" if not bad else ",".join(bad), "clean",
    "CONTROL=DEAD. Decoder match on the PLANTED literals: MSR-imm frame (w & 0x0FB0F000) == "
    "0x0320F000, field nibble (w>>16)&0xF = 0001 c-form / 1000 f-form, rot 0, bit 22 clear, imm8 "
    "== the key's mode.")
if bad:
    report_dead("enc ok")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1: live / pc adv
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(
    prologue() + plant(["mov_r0_2a", "str_r0"])
    + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST), "print pc"])
row("live", "0x%x" % (dumped(early, DEST) or 0) if alive else "dead", "0x2a",
    "CONTROL=DEAD. Plant, step and dump executed at all.")
row("pc adv", "0x%x" % (printed(early, "pc") or 0) if alive else "dead", "0x%x" % (CODE + 8),
    "CONTROL=DEAD. The words were translated as 4-byte ARM instructions.")
if rows[-2][1] != rows[-2][2] or rows[-1][1] != rows[-1][2]:
    report_dead("live/pc adv")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1b: armed
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(
    prologue() + plant(["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"])
    + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 3", dumpcmd(DEST, DEST + 8)])
a4, a8 = dumped(early, DEST + 4), dumped(early, DEST + 8)
armed = "%s/%s" % (("0x%x" % a4) if (alive and a4 is not None) else "?",
                   ("0x%x" % a8) if (alive and a8 is not None) else "?")
row("armed", armed, "0x%x/0x%x" % (BOOT_CPSR, BOOT_SP),
    "CONTROL=DEAD. SVC32 privileged testarm -- not USR32, not barearm, and not a debugger cpsr "
    "write. If this reds NO kill row may be scored.")
if armed != rows[-1][2]:
    report_dead("armed")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S2: no cbit
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["f_14", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("no cbit", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8), "m=13 sp",
    "MSR CPSR_f writes the FLAGS byte, so the mode field is untouched and the host must live. "
    "NOT CONTROL=DEAD: a red here is a decoder finding, not a reproduction.")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S3/S4: the kill rows
for nm, key, dec in (("kill 14", "c_14", 20), ("kill 15", "c_15", 21)):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds([key, "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
                     pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
        "MSR CPSR_c, #0x%02x into a RESERVED mode reaches arm_load_register_bank's default: and "
        "exit(1)s. PRE is `dead' -- EOF ALONE -- and that FAIL is the reproduction. #562: "
        "if=0 pins FIELD-ONLY (the write clears I/F; a whole-op decline keeps if=3)."
        % int(key[2:], 16))
    t = early + late
    diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, "load mode %d seen" % dec
                                 if ("arm_load_register_bank: unimplemented mode %d" % dec) in t
                                 else "NO LOAD TEXT"))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S5: save 2s
#  R3: the spec's slots -- post-MSR1 CPSR at DEST+4, return CPSR at DEST+8, SP at DEST+12.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_14", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "str_sp_c"],
                      [1, 2, 1, 3])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("save 2s", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 12, rt_at=DEST + 8),
    "m=13 sp once rt",
    "#0x14 then #0x13 in one process. On HEAD it dies on the FIRST MSR (the same Load death as "
    "kill 14). A Load-only fix still dies on the SECOND (Save of the reserved mode). Keep kill 15.")
t = early + late
diagnosis.append("save 2s: load=%s save=%s" % (
    "yes" if "arm_load_register_bank: unimplemented mode 20" in t else "no",
    "yes" if "arm_save_register_bank: unimplemented mode 20" in t else "no"))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S6..S13: accept side
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_10", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("acc usr", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8), "m=10 sp0",
    "USR32 is ONE-WAY (the USR mask strips the c-byte). SP is the USR bank's (0), so ` sp' in "
    "the got means the bank Load did not happen.")

for nm, key, want_m in (("acc fiq", "c_11", 0x11), ("acc irq", "c_12", 0x12),
                        ("acc abt", "c_17", 0x17), ("acc und", "c_1b", 0x1B),
                        ("acc sys", "c_1f", 0x1F)):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds([key, "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8",
                           "c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "str_sp_10"], [8],
                          late_end=DEST + 16)
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    if not marked or not arming(early, full):
        got = "unarmed"
    elif not alive:
        got = "dead"
    else:
        arrive, sp_arr = dumped(late, DEST + 4), dumped(late, DEST + 8)
        back, sp_back = dumped(late, DEST + 12), dumped(late, DEST + 16)
        got = "%s/%s/%s/%s" % (
            "m=%02x" % (arrive & 0x1F) if arrive not in (None, POISON) else "nofire",
            "0" if sp_arr == 0 else "x",
            "m=%02x" % (back & 0x1F) if back not in (None, POISON) else "nofire",
            "sp" if sp_back == BOOT_SP else "nosp")
    row(nm, got, "m=%02x/0/m=13/sp" % want_m,
        "ACCEPT + CANARY in one row: arrive mode/SP==0 (banked, BSS-zero), return mode 0x13 with "
        "SP==BOOT_SP. A Save no-op returns SP=0; a Load no-op arrives with SP=BOOT_SP.")

cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("same svc", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8), "m=13 sp",
    "SAME-MODE: Load is not called, so SP-UNCHANGED *IS* the preservation check. Not 0xd3 -- "
    "#0x13 clears I|F.")

cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_10", "c_14", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("usr mask", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full), "m=10",
    "In USR32 the c-byte is STRIPPED, so the following #0x14 must NOT reach the mode field. "
    "The host lives and the mode stays 0x10.")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 rows
#  kill 1e: pins the helper's DOMAIN past the two driven reserved values -- a range form
#  admitting 0x1c-0x1e sends 0x1e to Load's backstop and reads m=1e.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_1e", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("kill 1e", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
                        pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
    "MSR CPSR_c, #0x1e: unmodelled, like 0x14/0x15. Kills a helper widened by RANGE to 0x1e.")
diag_unimpl("kill 1e", early + late)

#  kill 03: the only driven mode value with bit 4 CLEAR.  Every other MSR value here has bit 4
#  set, so a decline that cleared only M[3:0] from the mask (`& ~0x0f') still wrote bit 4 = 0 from
#  this value and reached Load's backstop with mode 0x03; the rows before this one could not see it.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_03", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("kill 03", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
                        pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
    "MSR CPSR_c, #0x03 (bit 4 clear): declined, mode unchanged. Pins the full 5-bit mode mask.")
diag_unimpl("kill 03", early + late)

#  The two SPSR restores.  `MSR SPSR_c, #0x14' poisons spsr_svc (I=F=0, mode 0x14); the restore
#  must land at TGT (else `nofire') in SVC32 -- current mode kept -- with I/F FROM THE SPSR
#  (if=0; a restore that skipped the CPSR write keeps the boot if=3) and the SVC SP.
for nm, op, site in (("rst movs", "movs_pc_lr", "dpi S-to-pc"),
                     ("rst ldm", "ldm_r5_pc_s", "arm_pop")):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds(["s_14", op], [6], restore=True)
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key=site,
                     pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
        "MSR SPSR_c,#0x14 then %s: the restore reaches Load's default: (HEAD dead). Post-fix: "
        "at TGT, mode kept, I/F from the SPSR, `%s' announce." % (op, site))
    diag_unimpl(nm, early + late)

#  zero spsr: NO MSR at all -- spsr_svc is never written on testarm (cpu_new zero-fills), so
#  `MOVS pc,lr' from boot SVC32 restores mode 0x00.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["movs_pc_lr"], [4], restore=True)
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("zero spsr", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8,
                          once_key="dpi S-to-pc", pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
    "MOVS pc,lr from boot SVC32 with spsr_svc never written (mode 0x00). HEAD dead.")
diag_unimpl("zero spsr", early + late)

#  The restores' ACCEPT side: a modelled SPSR mode must be APPLIED.  A guard made unconditional
#  (always keep the current mode) reads m=13 here.  SYS32 SP is the usr/sys bank's (0).
for nm, op in (("acc movs", "movs_pc_lr"), ("acc ldm", "ldm_r5_pc_s")):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds(["s_1f", op], [6], restore=True)
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, pin_if=True),
        "m=1f if=0 sp0",
        "MSR SPSR_c,#0x1f then %s: SYS32 restored, SP from the usr/sys bank (0), no announce."
        % op)

#  The BACKSTOPS: reached only with an unmodelled mode already in CPSR, which only the debugger
#  `cpsr=' ASSIGNMENT can now install (no bank switch, no flags
#  sync).  NOT `put', which writes MEMORY.  The poke follows the arming dumps.  once_key names
#  a DECLINE site that must NOT announce: a plant is not a guest decline (dbg both: the DPI
#  guard runs only when an SPSR was copied, and mode 0x14 has none).
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4], poke=["cpsr=0xd4"])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("dbg save", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, backstops=True,
                         once_key="msr_imm"),
    "m=13 sp0 save",
    "cpsr=0xd4 then MSR CPSR_c,#0x13: Save's default: (HEAD exit(1)) announces and returns; "
    "Load(SVC) gives the never-saved svc SP: an actual 0, ` sp0'.")
diag_unimpl("dbg save", early + late)

cmds, full = msr_cmds(["movs_pc_lr"], [4], restore=True, poke=["cpsr=0xd4"])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("dbg both", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, backstops=True,
                         once_key="dpi S-to-pc"),
    "m=14 sp save load",
    "cpsr=0xd4 then MOVS pc,lr: no SPSR for 0x14, so BOTH Save's and Load's default: run in ONE "
    "instruction -- the only carrier of Load's backstop, and of their distinct latch bits.")
diag_unimpl("dbg both", early + late)

#  latch ind: ALL THREE guest declines in ONE process -- MSR CPSR_c,#0x14, then MSR SPSR_c,#0x14
#  and LDM {pc}^ (arm_pop) to TGT, whose MOVS pc,lr (DPI) restores the same poisoned spsr_svc to
#  TGT2.  Each site has its own mode_warned bit, so all three texts must appear; any two sharing a
#  bit print only the first.  (The other rows are each a fresh process and cannot see sharing.)
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_14", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"], [8], restore=True, chain=True)
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
#  once_key="register_bank": the generic ` once' token would be set by ANY of the three texts and
#  so carries nothing here; keyed on the backstops it pins their ABSENCE instead (no guest
#  decline may fall through to Save/Load).  The per-site ` msr pop dpi' tags are the channel.
row("latch ind", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, pin_if=True,
                          once_key="register_bank", declines=True), "m=13 if=0 sp msr pop dpi",
    "MSR, LDM^ and MOVS declines in ONE session: three distinct latch bits, three texts.")
diag_unimpl("latch ind", early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 pass 2: latch rpt
#  EVERY one of the five latch sites visited TWICE in ONE process.  Wants EXACTLY ONE announce per
#  site (a latch that never suppresses prints two; two sites sharing a bit print one between
#  them) AND the repair still applied on the second visit: the declines leave SVC32 (a=13/..),
#  the backstops leave the planted 0x14 alive (b=14/..).
#    CODE  c_14 c_15 (msr x2)  s_14  ldm r5 -> T1      T1  ldm r6 -> T2  (pop x2)
#    T2    movs pc,lr -> T3                            T3  add lr,#0x40 ; movs -> T4  (dpi x2)
#    T4    mrs ; str [r4,#4] ; str sp,[r4,#8]
#  then cpsr=0xd4, pc=T5, lr=T6:
#    T5    movs pc,lr -> T6  (save+load)   T6  add lr,#0x40 ; movs -> T7  (save+load again)
#    T7    mrs ; str [r4,#12] ; str sp,[r4,#16]
LR1, LR2, LR3, LR4, LR5, LR6, LR7 = 0x8100, 0x8140, 0x8180, 0x81C0, 0x8200, 0x8240, 0x8280
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
    ["c_14", "c_15", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"],
    blocks=[(LR1, ["ldm_r6_pc_s"]), (LR2, ["movs_pc_lr"]), (LR3, ["add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]),
            (LR4, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"]), (LR5, ["movs_pc_lr"]),
            (LR6, ["add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]), (LR7, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "str_sp_10"])],
    data={LDMW: LR1, LDMW + 4: LR2},
    regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "r6=0x%x" % (LDMW + 4), "lr=0x%x" % LR3],
    segments=(((), 11), (["cpsr=0xd4", "pc=0x%x" % LR5, "lr=0x%x" % LR6], 6)),
    late_end=DEST + 16)
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    def _half(ca, sa):
        c, v = dumped(late, ca), dumped(late, sa)
        if c in (None, POISON):
            return "nofire"
        return "%02x/%d/%s" % (c & 0x1F, (c >> 6) & 3,
                               "sp" if v == BOOT_SP else ("0" if v == 0 else "x"))
    t = early + late
    got = "a=%s b=%s msr%d pop%d dpi%d save%d load%d" % (
        _half(DEST + 4, DEST + 8), _half(DEST + 12, DEST + 16),
        announces(t, "msr_imm"), announces(t, "arm_pop"), announces(t, "dpi S-to-pc"),
        announces(t, "arm_save_register_bank"), announces(t, "arm_load_register_bank"))
row("latch rpt", got, "a=13/0/sp b=14/3/sp msr1 pop1 dpi1 save1 load1",
    "Five latch sites x2 in ONE process: exactly one announce each, repair kept on visit 2.")
diag_unimpl("latch rpt", early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 pass 2: src dpi / src pop
#  The SPSR SOURCE LABEL is a floor axis: FIQ/IRQ/ABT/UND/SVC each select a different spsr_* that
#  feeds new_cpsr in BOTH restore paths.  Per label and path, ONE session: enter the mode by a
#  modelled MSR, set its SPSR to N|Z|0x14 (reserved) and restore (DECLINED: mode kept, N Z from
#  the SPSR, I/F clear), then re-arm SPSR_c=#0x1f and restore again (ACCEPTED: 0xc000001f).  The
#  whole CPSR word is read back each time: N/Z pin "every other bit from the SPSR" and the cached
#  flags sync; a nonzero high byte pins the helper's `& ARM_FLAG_MODE' projection.
for lab, ck, mode in (("fiq", "c_11", 0x11), ("irq", "c_12", 0x12), ("abt", "c_17", 0x17),
                      ("und", "c_1b", 0x1B), ("svc", "c_13", 0x13)):
    for path in ("dpi", "pop"):
        if path == "dpi":
            early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
                [ck, "mov_lr_8100", "sf_c0", "s_14", "movs_pc_lr"],
                blocks=[(TGT, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "s_1f", "add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]),
                        (TGT + 0x40, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8"])],
                segments=(((), 12),))
            key = "dpi S-to-pc"
        else:
            early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
                [ck, "sf_c0", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"],
                blocks=[(TGT, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "s_1f", "ldm_r6_pc_s"]),
                        (TGT + 0x40, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8"])],
                data={LDMW: TGT, LDMW + 4: TGT + 0x40},
                regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "r6=0x%x" % (LDMW + 4)],
                segments=(((), 10),))
            key = "arm_pop"
        if not marked or not arming(early, full):
            got = "unarmed"
        elif not alive:
            got = "dead"
        else:
            c1, c2 = dumped(late, DEST + 4), dumped(late, DEST + 8)
            got = "%s %s%s" % (
                "nofire" if c1 in (None, POISON) else "%08x" % c1,
                "nofire" if c2 in (None, POISON) else "%08x" % c2,
                " once" if announces(early + late, key) == 1 else "")
        row("src %s %s" % (path, lab), got, "%08x %08x once" % (NZ | mode, NZ | 0x1F),
            "%s from %s: SPSR N|Z|0x14 declined (mode kept), then N|Z|0x1f accepted."
            % (path.upper(), lab.upper()))
        diag_unimpl("src %s %s" % (path, lab), early + late)

row("row count", len(rows) + 1, 38, "identity row (floor clause (c)): the row set is complete.")

print("ARMMSRMODE_CONTROL=OK")

    n += 1 if ok else 0
    if not ok:

+/*
+ *  arm_mode_is_modelled():
+ *
+ *  #562: 1 if M[4:0] of `mode' is one of the seven modes this model banks
+ *  (10 11 12 13 17 1b 1f), else 0.  Its case list MUST stay equal to the
+ *  case lists of arm_save_register_bank() and arm_load_register_bank()
+ *  below: the three switches together define "modelled", and those two
+ *  default: arms are reached only for a mode this returns 0 for.  Extern,
+ *  not static: the DPI template is compiled as its own object.
+ */
+int arm_mode_is_modelled(uint32_t mode)
+{
+	switch (mode & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
+	case ARM_MODE_USR32:
+	case ARM_MODE_SYS32:
+	case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
+	case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
+	case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
+	case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
+	case ARM_MODE_UND32:
+		return 1;
+	default:return 0;
+	}
+}

266: def printed(t, reg):
267:     #  COPIED VERBATIM from regress/lca_isalane_probe.py.  `print <reg>' emits a BARE `0x...'.
268:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
269:     return None if not m else int(m.group(1), 16)

271: 
272: def dumpcmd(addr, end=None):
273:     """*** ALWAYS DUMP FROM THE 16-BYTE-ALIGNED BASE. ***  debugger_cmd_dump does
274:     `addr = addr_start & ~0xf' and blank-pads groups before addr_start (arm_bdt_probe.py
275:     read_mem32(), measured 2026-09-11)."""
276:     base = addr & ~0xF
277:     top = ((end if end is not None else addr) + 16) & ~0xF
278:     if top <= base:
279:         top = base + 16
280:     return "dump 0x%x 0x%x" % (base, top)

282: 
283: def dumped(t, addr):
284:     """The word stored at `addr', from the LAST aligned dump line in `t' (R2: callers pass the
285:     early or the late half, never the whole transcript).  Memory-order bytes, little-endian
286:     guest: assemble like sw() in arm_flags_probe.py."""
287:     label = "0x%08x" % (addr & ~0xF)
288:     idx = (addr & 0xF) // 4
289:     val = None
290:     for line in t.splitlines():
291:         if line.startswith(label):
292:             parts = line[12:48].split()
293:             if len(parts) == 4 and len(parts[idx]) == 8:
294:                 val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(parts[idx]), "little")
295:     return val

297: 
298: def plant(ops, at=CODE):
299:     out = []
300:     for i, k in enumerate(ops):
301:         out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, WORDS[k]))
302:     out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), WORDS["b_self"]))
303:     return out
304: 
305: 
306: def prologue():
307:     """POISON DEST..DEST+16 (the post-MSR stores must overwrite them) and point r4 at DEST."""
308:     return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (DEST + o, POISON) for o in (0, 4, 8, 12, 16)] \
309:         + ["r4=0x%x" % DEST]

312: PREFIX = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3"]
313: 
314: 
315: def msr_cmds(ops, steps_after, late_end=DEST + 12, restore=False, regs=(), poke=(),
316:              chain=False):
317:     """Standard MSR session: prefix MRS/STR to DEST, arming dumps (DEST and the whole planted
318:     stream), MARK, the stimulus steps, then the result dump.
319:     #562: restore=True also plants TGT_OPS at TGT, sets lr=TGT and the LDMW word to TGT, and
320:     arms all three (a fall-through past the restore lands on b_self and reads `nofire').
321:     `regs' are debugger assignments made BEFORE the prefix; `poke' ones AFTER the arming dumps
322:     (a `cpsr=' poke must follow the prefix, whose MRS arms DEST == 0xd3).
323:     chain=True (with restore): TGT holds `movs pc,lr' and the readback TGT_OPS move to TGT2,
324:     where lr points -- so one session can run an LDM^ restore and then a MOVS restore."""
325:     full = PREFIX + ops
326:     cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
327:     extra = {}
328:     rb = TGT2 if chain else TGT
329:     if restore:
330:         cmds += plant(TGT_OPS, at=rb) + ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (LDMW, TGT),
331:                                          "lr=0x%x" % rb, "r5=0x%x" % LDMW]
332:         for i, k in enumerate(TGT_OPS + ["b_self"]):
333:             extra[rb + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
334:         extra[LDMW] = TGT
335:         if chain:
336:             cmds += plant(["movs_pc_lr"], at=TGT)
337:             extra[TGT] = WORDS["movs_pc_lr"]
338:             extra[TGT + 4] = WORDS["b_self"]
339:     cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
340:                           dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
341:     if restore:
342:         cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT, TGT + 4 * len(TGT_OPS)), dumpcmd(LDMW)]
343:         if chain:
344:             cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT2, TGT2 + 4 * len(TGT_OPS))]
345:     cmds += [MARK] + list(poke) + ["step %d" % s for s in steps_after] \
346:         + [dumpcmd(DEST, late_end)]
347:     return cmds, (full, extra)
348: 
349: 
350: def arming(early, full):
351:     """R4: DEST == 0xd3 after the prefix, and every planted word reads back as its WORDS entry.
352:     #562: `full' may be (ops, {addr: word}) -- the extra plants (restore target, LDM word)."""
353:     extra = {}
354:     if isinstance(full, tuple):
355:         full, extra = full
356:     if dumped(early, DEST) != BOOT_CPSR:
357:         return False
358:     for i, k in enumerate(full):
359:         if dumped(early, CODE + 4 * i) != WORDS[k]:
360:             return False
361:     for a, w in extra.items():
362:         if dumped(early, a) != w:
363:             return False
364:     return True
365: 
366: 
367: def prog_session(code_ops, blocks=(), data=None, regs=(), segments=((), ()), late_end=DEST + 12):
368:     """#562 pass 2: a multi-block program.  code_ops at CODE (after PREFIX), each (addr, ops) block
369:     at addr, each {addr: word} data word planted; every block and data word is ARMED (dumped and
370:     checked before MARK).  `segments' = ((pokes, steps), ...) after MARK: debugger assignments,
371:     then `step N'."""
372:     data = data or {}
373:     full = PREFIX + list(code_ops)
374:     cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
375:     extra = {}
376:     for a, ops in blocks:
377:         cmds += plant(ops, at=a)
378:         for i, k in enumerate(list(ops) + ["b_self"]):
379:             extra[a + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
380:     for a, w in data.items():
381:         cmds.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (a, w))
382:         extra[a] = w
383:     cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
384:                           dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
385:     for a, ops in blocks:
386:         cmds.append(dumpcmd(a, a + 4 * len(ops)))
387:     for a in data:
388:         cmds.append(dumpcmd(a))
389:     cmds.append(MARK)
390:     for pokes, steps in segments:
391:         cmds += list(pokes) + (["step %d" % steps] if steps else [])
392:     cmds.append(dumpcmd(DEST, late_end))
393:     early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
394:     return early, late, alive, marked, (full, extra)

396: 
397: def announces(t, key):
398:     """#562 pass 2: how MANY `(once)' lines the named site printed -- a latch that never
399:     suppresses prints twice when its site is visited twice; any() could not see that."""
400:     return sum(1 for ln in t.splitlines() if key in ln and "(once)" in ln)
401: 
402: 
403: def diag_unimpl(nm, t):
404:     """#562: which register-bank `unimplemented mode' text a session printed (HEAD: the
405:     function that exit(1)ed -- the reason under test; fixed: the backstop that announced)."""
406:     hits = sorted(set(re.findall(r"arm_(save|load)_register_bank: unimplemented mode (\d+)", t)))
407:     diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, ", ".join("%s mode %s" % h for h in hits) if hits
408:                                  else "no bank text"))
409: 
410: 
411: def announced(t, key):
412:     """#562: a `(once)' announce from the NAMED site -- the five sites share the token, so a
413:     bare `(once)' cannot tell a decline from a backstop, or Save's backstop from Load's."""
414:     return any(key in ln and "(once)" in ln for ln in t.splitlines())
415: 
416: 
417: def classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, cpsr_at=DEST + 4, sp_at=None, rt_at=None,
418:              once_key=None, pin_if=False, backstops=False, declines=False):
419:     """The got string.  EOF ALONE => dead.  Arming is checked first, from the EARLY half.
420:     #562: once_key names the announcing site; pin_if adds the CPSR I/F pair (field-only vs
421:     whole-operation decline); backstops adds ` save' / ` load' per Save/Load announce."""
422:     if not marked or not arming(early, full):
423:         return "unarmed"
424:     if not alive:
425:         return "dead"
426:     cpsr = dumped(late, cpsr_at)
427:     if cpsr is None or cpsr == POISON:
428:         return "nofire"
429:     got = "m=%02x" % (cpsr & 0x1F)
430:     if pin_if:
431:         got += " if=%d" % ((cpsr >> 6) & 3)
432:     if sp_at is not None:
433:         v = dumped(late, sp_at)
434:         #  #562 pass 2: ` sp0' is an ACTUAL zero readback (a never-loaded bank), not merely
435:         #  "not BOOT_SP" -- a missing or POISON slot now renders neither token.
436:         if v == BOOT_SP:
437:             got += " sp"
438:         elif v == 0:
439:             got += " sp0"
440:     t = early + late
441:     if (announced(t, once_key) if once_key else "(once)" in t):
442:         got += " once"
443:     if declines:
444:         for key, tag in (("msr_imm", "msr"), ("arm_pop", "pop"), ("dpi S-to-pc", "dpi")):
445:             if announced(t, key):
446:                 got += " " + tag
447:     if backstops:
448:         if announced(t, "arm_save_register_bank"):
449:             got += " save"
450:         if announced(t, "arm_load_register_bank"):
451:             got += " load"
452:     if rt_at is not None:
453:         v = dumped(late, rt_at)
454:         if v not in (None, POISON) and (v & 0x1F) == 0x13:
455:             got += " rt"
456:     return got
457: 
458: 
459: def report_dead(why):
460:     print("arm_msrmode_probe -- binary=%s label=%s machine=testarm" % (BIN, LABEL))
461:     print("ARMMSRMODE_CONTROL=DEAD")
462:     print("ARMMSRMODE_RESULT=0/0")
463:     print("  *** SETUP FAILED (%s) -- the vehicle is not the vehicle.  NOT 'no defect'. ***" % why)
464:     for nm, got, want, note in rows:
465:         print("  %-11s got=%-16s want=%-16s %s" % (nm, got, want, note[:56]))
466:     print("ARMMSRMODE_DONE")
467:     raise SystemExit(0)

1331: > The detector passed (a), (b) and (c) and was still blind to a ONE-CHARACTER edit that
1332: > re-opened the very defect the round had just closed. (b) restores the DEFECT; (d) neuters
1333: > the FIX. A detector can be sensitive to one and blind to the other, because they fail at
1334: > different sites: (b) fails where the old code was, (d) fails where the new code is.
1335: >
1336: > **HOW TO BUILD THE (d) MUTANT �?" and it is the same discriminator the accept-row rule
1337: > already uses.** Take each predicate the fix **DEPENDS ON** and weaken it to the
1338: > least-restrictive form that still *reads* like the same check: a mask narrowed
1339: > (`& 3` -> `& 1`), a condition qualified (`if (X)` -> `if (X && writeflag == MEM_WRITE)`),
1340: > a bound relaxed. **Not deletion** �?" deletion is (b) wearing (d)'s clothes and is usually
1341: > already covered. The test is whether a plausible future edit could produce it.
1342: >
1343: > **WHY THE ONE-CHARACTER FORM IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS.** `dma alias`, the row written

1346: > A row written from the FIX's intent tests the intent, not the boundary �?" so (d)'s mutant
1347: > has to come from an adversary, which is exactly what the ask-again buys.
1348: >
1349: > **COST: one mutant per repair predicate**, the same order as the accept-row rule (#448 = 4,

1351: > the predicates are the ones the fix ADDED, and that list is finite and visible.

1353: > ### *** "THE PREDICATE THE FIX INTRODUCED" ENUMERATED THE EMPTY SET FOR MOST OF THIS

1356: >
1357: > Clause (d) says *keep the guard, null its repair*. As written it enumerated only
1358: > predicates the diff ADDS -- and **the two commonest fix shapes here add none**:
1359: >
1360: > * a **pure deletion** introduces no predicate at all;
1361: > * a **DE-FATALISATION** introduces none either -- it changes what an EXISTING
1362: >   predicate's branch does. That is most of this project's queue.
1363: >
1364: > So for those rounds clause (d) enumerated **nothing**, and a round could satisfy the
1365: > floor while having built no (d) mutant whatever. `#461` measured (d) successfully only
1366: > because its fix happened to ADD a predicate (an alignment mask) for the mutant to weaken.
1367: >
1368: > **Reading it as DEPENDS ON fixes both cases at once**, and it is why this now replaces a
1369: > narrower deletion-only rule written earlier the same day: that rule was a special case of
1370: > this gap, and two overlapping statements of one rule is the accumulation this carrier is
1371: > supposed to refuse.
1372: >
1373: > **The two shapes, once the enumeration is right:**
1374: > * **fix ADDS a predicate** -> weaken the added one (unchanged; the original rule).
1375: > * **fix adds none** -> the (d) mutant reroutes an EXISTING guard's repair. For a
1376: >   de-fatalisation: **swap the guard's operand for a field the guarded BODY reads.**
1377: >   For a pure deletion: **re-introduce the removed behaviour under a DIFFERENT predicate**
1378: >   -- not the same one widened, which is (b) wearing (d)'s clothes.
1379: >
1380: > *** THE MEASURED CASE, and it is why this matters rather than being tidy: ***
1381: > `#462` de-fatalised two arms. Its guard reads `d->data`; the body it guards reads
1382: > `d->data` AND `d->data_len`; the two are set together in one place and cleared in two
1383: > places that touch only ONE of them. Swapping the guard's operand to `d->data_len == 0`
1384: > **scored a CLEAN SWEEP on a 52-row detector that had just passed twelve mutants**, and
1385: > restored a NULL-pointer read of 408 bytes. Under the old wording that was coverage debt;
1386: > under DEPENDS ON it is a **floor breach, L2, fix in-round** -- which is what the round
1387: > did, before the wording was corrected.
1388: >
1389: > #### *** THE BOUND, and it is not optional: "DEPENDS ON" INVITES TRANSITIVE DATA-FLOW
1390: > #### CHASING, which is unbounded and is how this becomes `#392`. Named 2026-08-24 by the
1391: > #### adjudicating seat, in the same breath as upholding the correction. ***
1392: >
1393: > > **DEPENDS ON enumerates exactly two things: (1) the predicates DOMINATING the diff's
1394: > > edited lines, walked up only to the ENCLOSING FUNCTION BOUNDARY -- never into callees
1395: > > or callers; and (2) for operand-swap forms, swaps drawn ONLY from fields the guarded
1396: > > BODY reads (the guard-field/body-field grep the pre-table check already mandates).**
1397: >
1398: > That is finite, derivable from the diff by reading, and **it provably contains the
1399: > measured case**: the `d->data == NULL` guard dominates the edited decline (clause 1), and
1400: > `data_len` is a body-read field (clause 2), so `M11` falls out mechanically rather than
1401: > by inspiration. It cannot become `#392` because the enumeration stays closed over the
1402: > diff's own hunks -- the same closure the INTRODUCED wording had, without the empty-set
1403: > defect that made INTRODUCED enumerate nothing for a de-fatalisation.

1465: > > the single value being written are single-operand MARGINALS, not floor; joint cells outside the floor

#ifdef quick_pc_to_pointers
#undef quick_pc_to_pointers
#endif

#ifdef MODE32
#define	quick_pc_to_pointers(cpu) {					\
	uint32_t pc_tmp32 = cpu->pc;					\
	struct DYNTRANS_TC_PHYSPAGE *ppp_tmp;				\
	ppp_tmp = cpu->cd.DYNTRANS_ARCH.phys_page[pc_tmp32 >> 12];	\
	if (ppp_tmp != NULL) {						\
		cpu->cd.DYNTRANS_ARCH.cur_ic_page = &ppp_tmp->ics[0];	\
		cpu->cd.DYNTRANS_ARCH.next_ic =				\
		    cpu->cd.DYNTRANS_ARCH.cur_ic_page +			\
		    DYNTRANS_PC_TO_IC_ENTRY(pc_tmp32);			\
	} else								\
		DYNTRANS_PC_TO_POINTERS(cpu);				\
}

#ifndef quick_pc_to_pointers_arm
#define	quick_pc_to_pointers_arm(cpu) {					\
	if (cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_T) {				\
		cpu->cd.arm.next_ic = &nothing_call;			\
	} else								\
		quick_pc_to_pointers(cpu);				\
}
#endif

#else
#define quick_pc_to_pointers(cpu)	DYNTRANS_PC_TO_POINTERS(cpu)
#endif

20335: ### `armmsrmodekill` (HIGH) -- filed 2026-09-17. **A PRIVILEGED GUEST `MSR` ENDS THE EMULATOR
20336: ### PROCESS.** Volunteered by the grok seat while it was refuting a DIFFERENT brief, then
20337: ### CONFIRMED on a second firing by a full instruction-to-`switch` trace.
20338: 
20339: **`cpu_arm.c` `arm_load_register_bank()` `default:` -> `fatal(); exit(1)` on any reserved
20340: `M[4:0]`.**  One ARM instruction from the boot mode kills the host:
20341: 
20342:     MSR CPSR_c, #0x14        encoding 0xE321F014, on -E testarm
20343: 
20344: `testarm` boots **SVC32 (`0x13`), privileged** (`arm_cpu_new` sets `ARM_MODE_SVC32` on both the
20345: prom and non-prom arms).  `0x14` is the first hole above it and is none of the seven modelled
20346: modes (USR32/SYS32/FIQ32/IRQ32/SVC32/ABT32/UND32).  **There is no `arm_mode_ok` anywhere.**
20347: 
20348: *** GATE 14's 358 REAL-DECODE CHECKS NEVER FEED THIS ENCODING. ***  That is the gate blind-spot
20349: this row is ranked on.
20350: 
20351: ## THE CHAIN, AND THE THREE THINGS THAT LOOK LIKE FILTERS AND ARE NOT
20352: 
20353: `X(msr_imm)` masks to flags **only when the CURRENT mode is exactly `ARM_MODE_USR32`** -- SYS32 is
20354: not in that test, and boot is SVC32, so it does not fire.  The same-mode skip does not fire
20355: (`0x13 != 0x14`).  The c-bit is set, so the mode field is writable.  The store is then
20356: **unfiltered**, and `arm_load_register_bank()` falls to `default:` and calls `exit(1)`.
20357: 
20358: *** DO NOT WITNESS WITH `MSR CPSR_c, #0`.  IT IS THE `#449`-SHAPE TRAP. ***  DDI 0100I's prose
20359: (A2.5.1) makes M[4:0] privileged while the EXTRACTED Table A4-1 shows `PrivMask = 0x0000000F`
20360: (a stagger hazard -- see the extracted-table calibration rule).  **Under the `0x0F` reading `#0`
20361: would keep M4 and land on USR32 `0x10`, which IS modelled and does NOT kill** -- so a `#0` probe
20362: could go GREEN ON A LIVE DEFECT.  `0x14` survives both readings, because M4 is already 1 from
20363: SVC32 and bits 3:0 of `0x14` are `0x4`.  Also usable: `0x15/0x16/0x18/0x19/0x1a/0x1c/0x1d/0x1e`.
20364: 
20365: #### *** REASON CORRECTED (MSRZERO-REASON-CORRECTION), SAME SESSION.  THE ADVICE ABOVE SURVIVES; THE
20366: #### REASONING FOR IT IS WRONG ON THIS EMULATOR. ***
20367: 
20368: The paragraph above says `#0` "could go GREEN ON A LIVE DEFECT" -- a `#449`-shape fake-green.
20369: **Measured against the source: it does not.**  `ARM_MODE_USR26` is `0x00`
20370: (`src/include/cpu_arm.h:104`), `arm_load_register_bank()` cases on exactly the seven **32-bit**
20371: modes, and `grep -c 'case ARM_MODE_USR26'` across `src/` is **ZERO**.  So `#0` writes mode 0,
20372: reaches the same `default:`, and **kills the host exactly as `0x14` does.**  There is no fake-green.
20373: 
20374: > **STILL DO NOT WITNESS WITH `#0`, FOR THE OTHER REASON: it rows the 26-BIT hole, not the
20375: > RESERVED 32-BIT hole.**  The defect under test is a reserved `M[4:0]` in a 32-bit-mode CPU; mode 0
20376: > is a different (and architecturally distinct) unmodelled case that happens to share the arm.
20377: > `0x14` is the first reserved hole above SVC32 and is the right stimulus.
20378: 
20379: *The `PrivMask = 0x0F` hazard from the EXTRACTED Table A4-1 is real as a READING hazard and is why
20380: `0x14` was chosen -- but it does not produce a green run here, because the value it would land on
20381: (`0x10`, USR32) is only reachable if the mask actually applies, and this emulator's store does not
20382: apply it.  I stated a trap that the fork's own code does not have.*
20383: 
20384: **This is the advice-survives-the-exemplar pattern the carrier documents**: the instruction held,
20385: the reason did not, and a wrong reason gets reused.  Caught by the seat that had supplied the
20386: original finding, when asked to break its own claim.
20387: 
20388: **Architecture, checked rather than assumed:** A2.5.7 / A4.1.39 -- a reserved `M[4:0]` is
20389: **UNPREDICTABLE**, and MSR lists **Exceptions: None**.  Real hardware neither takes an undefined
20390: exception nor stops.  *UNPREDICTABLE permits a no-op; it does not permit `exit(1)`.*
20391: 
20392: ## DISPOSITION: ONE ROUND, MEMBER 3 (DECLINE + LATCHED ANNOUNCE) AT BOTH `default:` ARMS
20393: 
20394: *** SAVE (`:559`) IS **NOT** FIRST-HIT REACHABLE, AND THAT DOES NOT SPLIT THE ROUND. ***
20395: `arm_save_register_bank()` runs BEFORE the write, on the OLD mode -- from boot that is SVC32, a
20396: modelled case, so it returns normally.  **The process dies in LOAD.**  Save's `default:` becomes
20397: reachable only once Load has installed a reserved mode and returned, which today never happens.
20398: **So a LOAD-ONLY fix CREATES the Save kill** -- sibling parity, one round, not a second row.
