VERDICT: DO-NOT-SHIP | 5 floor breaches

I count three uncovered enclosing predicates and one label/latch closure breach per restore path. These are **static detector findings**, not newly measured escapes. I wrote, built, and executed nothing.

1 | **Yes. A single-token example is changing `if (return_flag)` to `if (1)` in [arm_pop](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr.c:1799).** Every probe invocation of `arm_pop` is an S+PC restore, so this change is predicted to leave all 38 results unchanged. Ordinary loads would incorrectly perform SPSR restoration.

The five findings are:

| Breach | Uncovered behavior |
|---|---|
| POP `return_flag` | No non-return load checks that restoration stays disabled. |
| DPI destination-PC predicate | No instruction reaches the `A__PC` template with a general-register destination. Making [the comparison](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr_dpi.c:208) unconditional is predicted to escape. |
| DPI S predicate | No generic non-S DPI-to-PC instruction checks that [SPSR restoration](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr_dpi.c:216) stays disabled. |
| DPI label × latch | Non-SVC second declines and first-operation accepts are absent. |
| POP label × latch | Same gap independently. |

The DPI distinction matters: ordinary non-PC instructions in the probe select **different template instances**, so they do not exercise the false side of the destination-PC comparison. The [decoder selection](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr.c:4602) establishes this.

These enclosing predicates belong in this review under the recorded [DEPENDS-ON boundary](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/tools/CLAUDE.md:1393), despite predating the added lines.

The bounded static candidate sweep was **89 variants**:

| Family considered | Count |
|---|---:|
| MSR guard atoms/polarity and USR masking | 4 |
| POP/DPI mode-guard forced forms | 4 |
| `spsr_copied`: removed gate, initialization false, default true | 3 |
| Three enclosing-predicate widenings above | 3 |
| Helper: constant answers, projection removal, seven legal-case removals | 10 |
| Restore guard restricted/unconditional at each label, both paths | 20 |
| Label-conditioned repair suppression after warning / erroneous refusal before warning | 20 |
| Five sites × AND substitution, writer deletion, repair only before warning | 15 |
| All unordered latch-bit alias pairs | 10 |

Static predictions: **70 have distinguishing existing rows; 19 escape**—three enclosing-predicate variants and sixteen label/state variants. This is a reasoning denominator, **not an executed mutation table**.

2 | **The proposed 20-cell product omits latch state.** The reachable product is five labels × two paths × two SPSR outcomes × two site-latch states = **40 cells**.

| SPSR outcome / latch on entry | Covered | Missing |
|---|---:|---:|
| Declined / clear | 10 | 0 |
| Declined / set | 2, SVC only | 8 |
| Accepted / clear | 2, SVC only | 8 |
| Accepted / set | 10 | 0 |
| **Total** | **24** | **16** |

The [source rows](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/arm_msrmode_probe.py:762) always decline first, then accept. `latch rpt` supplies repeated declines only from SVC.

The eight missing **declined/set** cells alone establish the two restore-path breaches: applying repair only while unwarned at a non-SVC label changes none of the current executions. The latch is explicitly tested by the repair, so this is required state coverage under the [joint-state rule](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/tools/CLAUDE.md:1462).

Zero SPSR is different. The existing helper does not distinguish zero from other members of its unmodelled default class. Its absence from POP and other labels is value-coverage debt, not automatically another floor axis. It should nevertheless be disclosed **per path**.

3 | **The scores reconcile; several claims need correction.**

- **Counts:** Existing row records support fixed **38/38**, HEAD **16/38**, exactly **22 named reds**, and completion markers. Table 2 contains **36 failing mutants** on 37 rows. Table 3 contains **six failing mutants** on 38 rows, with the old-probe mask escape reproduced. “Zero escapes” holds for those measured tables; it does not establish closure.
- **Backstops:** Save’s description correctly admits later guest execution after a debugger plant. Load’s parenthetical remains misleading: a valid MSR installs a modelled mode, and [exception entry replaces the mode](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm.c:743) before Load. Those reach Save’s default after a plant, not Load’s default. The demonstrated Load carrier is DPI S-to-PC with no copied SPSR, preserving the planted invalid mode.
- **DPI T comment:** The stated ordering is correct: Load occurs before the odd-PC T assignment. Pointer selection intervenes; this does not establish execution-state coherence.
- **NOT CLAIMED:** It omits covered `0x03` and falsely includes it in the supposedly escaping `0x01–0x0f` range. The helper is exercised on **12/32 low-five-bit values**: seven modelled values plus `00,03,14,15,1e`.
- **I/F contract overclaim:** Every driven declined SPSR has **I=F=0**. Forcing I to zero in either restore repair is predicted to preserve every result. Reading the whole word cannot distinguish copying zero from forcing zero. Non-SVC source rows also enter with I/F already clear, so they do not independently prove I/F provenance.
- **Stale records:** `dbg both` is no longer the “only carrier” of Load or latch independence; `latch rpt` also carries them. PRETABLE2’s 37-row references to `run_fixed.out`/`run_head.out` now resolve to 38-row files.
- **Chronology:** Present filesystem timestamps do not independently establish PRETABLE’s claimed creation/addendum times: the relevant files share an import-time timestamp. The logs preserve table start times, not proof of the preceding writes.

4 | **The disclosure is useful but incomplete.** Unlisted mode values, repeated cached translations, per-CPU isolation, and T-state coherence are coverage limitations. Some are guest-reachable portions of the advertised policy; disclosure does not remove the product obligation. I found no current-code violation of the mode whitelist or local-mask policy in this static review.

The list also fails to disclose path-specific gaps: zero SPSR is covered only through DPI, while `0x15` and `0x1e` are driven through MSR, not both restores. The I/F preservation overclaim needs narrowing or additional evidence. These value gaps are separate from the mandatory predicate/state holes above.

Before shipping, measure the three missing enclosing-predicate controls and the sixteen missing restore label/state cells against fixed and corresponding mutations. For the non-S DPI control, **`MOV pc,lr` is insufficient**: the [decoder shortcuts it to `ret`](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/cc/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/cpu_arm_instr.c:4431). Use a control that actually reaches the generic DPI template.
